2.         Set request’s [origin](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") to request’s   [client](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [origin](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").  


-       If request’s [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is "`client`":  


1.         If request’s [client](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is non-null, then set   request’s [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to a   [clone](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of request’s [client](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s   [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). [[HTML]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   

2.         Otherwise, set request’s [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to a new   [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").  


### 4.1. Main fetch


To *main fetch*, given a [fetch params](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")fetchParams
and an optional boolean recursive (default false), run thesesteps:


1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

2.       Let response be null.

3.       If request’s [local-URLs-only flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is set and request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is not [local](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then set response to a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

4.       Run [report Content Security Policy violations for request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

5.       [Upgrade request to a potentially trustworthy URL, if appropriate](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

6.       [Upgrade a mixed content request to a potentially trustworthy URL, if appropriate](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

7.       If [should request be blocked due to a bad port](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [should fetching request be blocked as mixed content](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [should request be blocked by Content Security Policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), or [should request be blocked by Integrity Policy Policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") returns **blocked**, then set response to a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

8.       If request’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is the empty string, then set request’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to request’s [policy container](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").

9.       If request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is not "`no-referrer`", then set  request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to the result of invoking  [determine request’s referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). [[REFERRER]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")  

	As stated in *Referrer Policy*, user agents can provide the end user with  options to override request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to "`no-referrer`"  or have it expose less sensitive information.

10.       Set request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [scheme](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to  "`https`" if all of the following conditions are true:  
11. request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [scheme](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is   "`http`"          
12. request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [host](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is a   [domain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")          
13. request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [host](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s   [public suffix](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is not "`localhost`" or "`localhost.`"          
14. Matching request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [host](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") per   [Known HSTS Host Domain Name Matching](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   results in either a superdomain match with an asserted `includeSubDomains` directive   or a congruent match (with or without an asserted `includeSubDomains` directive) [[HSTS]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"); or   DNS resolution for the request finds a matching HTTPS RR per   [section 9.5](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   of [[SVCB]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   [[HSTS]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [[SVCB]](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")        

As all DNS operations are generally [implementation-defined](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), how it is  determined that DNS resolution contains an HTTPS RR is also [implementation-defined](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle"). As DNS  operations are not traditionally performed until attempting to [obtain a connection](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key"), user  agents might need to perform DNS operations earlier, consult local DNS caches, or wait until later  in the fetch algorithm and potentially unwind logic on discovering the need to change
request’s [current URL](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key")’s [scheme](/commit-snapshots/394d20d144ed1401c2c0e02c35bc3608cb2a2269/ "You can also press the 'y' key").

15.       
If recursive is false, then run the remaining steps [in parallel](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "Japanese").

16.       
If response is null, then set response to the result of running the steps  corresponding
to the first matching statement:  
fetchParams’s [preloaded response candidate](https://htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Simplified Chinese") is non-null           


1.           Wait until fetchParams’s     [preloaded response candidate](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`pending`".     

2.           [Assert](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3"): fetchParams’s     [preloaded response candidate](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") is a [response](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

3.           Return fetchParams’s [preloaded response candidate](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3").    


request’s [current URL](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [origin](#methods "Content Security Policy Level 3") is   [same origin](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3") with
request’s [origin](#bodies "Content Security Policy Level 3"), and request’s
[response tainting](#requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`basic`"       
request’s [current URL](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [scheme](#authentication-entries "Content Security Policy Level 3") is  
"`data`"          request’s [mode](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3") is   "`navigate`",
"`websocket`" or "`webtransport`"                 

1.           Set request’s     [response tainting](#network-partition-keys "Content Security Policy Level 3") to "`basic`".     

2.           Return the result of running [override fetch](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") given "`scheme-fetch`"     and fetchParams.    


HTML assigns any documents and workers created from [URLs](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose    [scheme](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3") is
"`data`" a unique [opaque origin](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3"). Service workers can    only be created from [URLs](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose [scheme](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") is an [HTTP(S) scheme](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3").    [[HTML]](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") [[SW]](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3")   
request’s [mode](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`same-origin`"

Return a [network error](#http-requests "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
request’s [mode](#http-responses "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`no-cors`"


1.           If request’s [redirect mode](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`follow`",     then return a [network error](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

2.           Set request’s [response tainting](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") to "`opaque`".     

3.           Return the result of running [override fetch](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") given "`scheme-fetch`" and     fetchParams.    


request’s [current URL](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [scheme](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not an   [HTTP(S) scheme](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")           

Return a [network error](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3").   
request’s [use-CORS-preflight flag](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") is set         
request’s [unsafe-request flag](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") is set and either
request’s   [method](#override-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not a [CORS-safelisted method](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") or   [CORS-unsafe request-header names](#http-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") with
request’s [header list](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")   [is not empty](#http-network-or-cache-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")           


1.           Set request’s     [response tainting](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     "`cors`".     

2.           Let corsWithPreflightResponse be the result of running [override fetch](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3")     given "`http-fetch`", fetchParams, and true.     

3.           If corsWithPreflightResponse is a [network error](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then     [clear cache entries](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3") using request.     

4.           Return corsWithPreflightResponse.    


Otherwise                 

1.           Set request’s     [response tainting](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     "`cors`".     

2.           Return the result of running [override fetch](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") given "`http-fetch`" and     fetchParams.    


-       
If recursive is true, then return response.

-       
If response is not a [network error](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") and
response is not a  [filtered response](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then:  


1.         If request’s [response tainting](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`cors`", then:    


1.           Let headerNames be the result of [extracting header list values](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3") given     \`[`Access-Control-Expose-Headers`](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3")\` and response’s     [header list](#websocket-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

2.           If request’s [credentials mode](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not     "`include`" and headerNames contains \``*`\`, then set     response’s [CORS-exposed header-name list](#fetch-elsewhere-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to all unique     [header](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") [names](#acknowledgments "Content Security Policy Level 3") in response’s     [header list](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

3.           Otherwise, if headerNames is non-null or failure, then set response’s      [CORS-exposed header-name list](#index-defined-here "Content Security Policy Level 3") to headerNames.      

	One of the headerNames can still be \``*`\` at this point,      but will only match a [header](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose [name](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3") is \``*`\`.    


-         Set response to the following [filtered response](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3") with response as    its [internal response](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3"), depending on request’s    [response tainting](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3"):    
"`basic`"              [basic filtered response](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Content Security Policy Level 3")              "`cors`"              [CORS filtered response](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly")              "`opaque`"              [opaque filtered response](#biblio-upgrade-insecure-requests "Upgrade Insecure Requests")                  


-       
Let internalResponse be response, if response is a [network error](#http-origin "The Web Origin Concept"); otherwise
response’s [internal response](#biblio-origin "The Web Origin Concept").

-       
If internalResponse’s [URL list](#preface "Infra Standard") [is empty](#preface "Infra Standard"), then  set it to a [clone](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") of
request’s [URL list](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard").  

A [response](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")’s [URL list](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") can be empty, e.g., when  fetching an `about:`
URL.

-       
Set internalResponse’s [redirect taint](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") to request’s
[redirect-taint](#infrastructure "Infra Standard").

-       
If request is a [navigation request](#infrastructure "Infra Standard"), then set
internalResponse’s [navigation timing allow values list](#biblio-infra "Infra Standard") to a [clone](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF") of
request’s [navigation timing allow values list](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF").

-       
If request’s [timing allow failed flag](#biblio-encoding "Encoding Standard") is unset, then set
internalResponse’s [timing allow passed flag](#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics").

-       
If response is not a [network error](#biblio-streams "Streams Standard") and any of the following returns
**blocked**  

-         [should internalResponse to request be blocked as mixed content](#biblio-websockets "WebSockets Standard")   

-         [should internalResponse to request be blocked by Content Security Policy](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF")   

-         [should internalResponse to request be blocked due to its MIME type](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism")   

-         [should internalResponse to request be blocked due to nosniff](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism")  


then set response and internalResponse to a [network error](#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics").

-       
If response’s [type](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") is "`opaque`",
internalResponse’s [status](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing") is a [range status](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing"),
internalResponse’s [range-requested flag](#concept-request "Resource Timing") is set, and
request’s [header list](#concept-request "Resource Timing") [does not contain](#concept-request "Resource Timing")
\``Range`\`, then set response and internalResponse to a  [network error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").  


Traditionally, APIs accept a ranged response even if a range was not requested. This
prevents   a partial response or a range not satisfiable response from an earlier
ranged request being   provided to an API that did not make a range request.   
Further details        
The above steps prevent the following attack:    

A media element is used to request a range of a cross-origin HTML resource. Although
this is    invalid media, a reference to a clone of the response can be retained
in a service worker. This    can later be used as the response to a script element’s
fetch. If the partial response is valid    JavaScript (even though the whole resource
is not), executing it would leak private data.   

-       
If response is not a [network error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") and  either
request’s [method](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is
\``HEAD`\` or \``CONNECT`\`, or internalResponse’s  [status](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is a [null body status](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"),  set
internalResponse’s [body](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") to  null and disregard any enqueuing toward it (if any).  

This standardizes the error handling for servers that violate HTTP.

-       
If request’s [integrity metadata](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is not the empty string, then:  


1.         Let processBodyError be this step: run [fetch response handover](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") given   fetchParams and a [network error](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").   

2.         If response’s [body](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is null, then run   processBodyError and abort these steps.   

3.         Let processBody given bytes be these steps:    


1.           If bytes do not [match](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")     request’s [integrity metadata](#concept-response "Resource Timing"), then run     processBodyError and abort these steps. [[SRI]](#concept-response "Resource Timing")     

2.           Set response’s [body](#concept-response "Resource Timing") to bytes     [as a body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing").     

3.           Run [fetch response handover](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") given fetchParams and response.    


-         [Fully read](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing") response’s [body](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") given   processBody and processBodyError.  


-       
Otherwise, run [fetch response handover](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") given fetchParams
and response.


---


The *fetch response handover*, given a [fetch params](#fetch-timing-info "Resource
Timing")fetchParams and a [response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")
response, run these steps:


1.       Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").

2.       If response is not a [network error](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")  and fetchParams’s  [request](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")’s [client](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") is a [secure context](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing"), then set  timingInfo’s [server-timing headers](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to the  result of [getting, decoding, and splitting](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") \``Server-Timing`\`  from response’s [internal response](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s  [header list](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").  

	Using _response_’s [internal response](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is safe as  exposing \``Server-Timing`\` header data is guarded through the  \``Timing-Allow-Origin`\` header.  

	The user agent may decide to expose \``Server-Timing`\` headers to non-secure contexts  requests as well.

3.       If fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing")’s [destination](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing") is "`document`", then set fetchParams’s [controller](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing")’s [full timing info](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to fetchParams’s [timing info](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

4.       Let processResponseEndOfBody be the following steps:  


1.         Let unsafeEndTime be the [unsafe shared current time](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").   

2.         Set fetchParams’s [controller](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing")’s    [report timing steps](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing") to the following steps given a    [global object](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing") global:    


1.           If fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s [URL](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s     [scheme](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is not an [HTTP(S) scheme](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing"), then return.     

2.           Set timingInfo’s [end time](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing") to the     [relative high resolution time](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing") given unsafeEndTime and     global.     

3.           Let cacheState be response’s [cache state](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").     

4.           Let bodyInfo be response’s [body info](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").     

5.           If response’s [timing allow passed flag](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") is not set,      then set timingInfo to the result of [creating an opaque timing info](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") for      timingInfo and set cacheState to the empty string.      

	This covers the case of response being a [network error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing").     

6.           Let responseStatus be 0.     

7.           If fetchParams’s [request](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing")’s [mode](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") is      not "`navigate`" or response’s      [redirect taint](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing") is "`same-origin`":      


1.             Set responseStatus to response’s [status](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing").       

2.             Let mimeType be the result of       [extracting a MIME type](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") from response’s       [header list](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing").       

3.             If mimeType is not failure, then set bodyInfo’s       [content type](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm "Resource Timing") to the result of       [minimizing a supported MIME type](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") given mimeType.      


-           If fetchParams’s [request](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing")’s     [initiator type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing") is non-null, then [mark resource timing](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing") given     timingInfo, fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s     [URL](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s     [initiator type](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), global, cacheState,     bodyInfo, and responseStatus.    


-         Let processResponseEndOfBodyTask be the following steps:    


1.           Set fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-params "Resource Timing")’s     [done flag](#fetch-params "Resource Timing").     

2.           If fetchParams’s [process response end-of-body](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") is     non-null, then run fetchParams’s     [process response end-of-body](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") given response.     

3.           If fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing")’s     [initiator type](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") is non-null and fetchParams’s     [request](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing")’s [client](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")’s     [global object](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") is fetchParams’s     [task destination](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing"), then run fetchParams’s     [controller](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")’s [report timing steps](#biblio-resource-timing "Resource Timing") given     fetchParams’s [request](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Navigation Timing")’s [client](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")’s     [global object](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").    


-         [Queue a fetch task](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to run processResponseEndOfBodyTask with   fetchParams’s [task destination](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").  


-       If fetchParams’s [process response](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") is non-null, then [queue a fetch task](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to run fetchParams’s [process response](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") given response, with fetchParams’s [task destination](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       Let internalResponse be response, if response is a [network error](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"); otherwise response’s [internal response](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       If response is a [network error](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"), then run the [WebDriver BiDi fetch error](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") steps with request. Otherwise, run the [WebDriver BiDi response completed](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") steps with request and response.

-       If internalResponse’s [body](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") is null, then run processResponseEndOfBody.

-       Otherwise:


1.         Let transformStream be a new `[TransformStream](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing")`.   

2.         Let identityTransformAlgorithm be an algorithm which, given chunk,   [enqueues](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") chunk in transformStream.   

3.         [Set up](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") transformStream with   [*transformAlgorithm*](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing") set to   identityTransformAlgorithm and   [*flushAlgorithm*](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing") set to   processResponseEndOfBody.   

4.         Set internalResponse’s [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")’s [stream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") to the   result of internalResponse’s [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")’s [stream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")   [piped through](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing") transformStream.  


This `[TransformStream](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing")` is needed for the purpose of receiving a notification when  the stream reaches its end, and is otherwise an [identity transform stream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy").

-       If fetchParams’s [process response consume body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is  non-null, then:  


1.         Let processBody given nullOrBytes be this step: run   fetchParams’s [process response consume body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") given   response and nullOrBytes.   

2.         Let processBodyError be this step: run fetchParams’s   [process response consume body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") given response and failure.   

3.         If internalResponse’s [body](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") is null, then   [queue a fetch task](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") to run processBody given null, with fetchParams’s   [task destination](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy").   

4.         Otherwise, [fully read](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") internalResponse’s   [body](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy") given processBody, processBodyError, and   fetchParams’s [task destination](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy").  


### 4.2. Override fetch


To *override fetch*, given "`scheme-fetch`" or"`http-fetch`" type, a [fetch params](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy")
fetchParams, andan optional boolean makeCORSPreflight (default false):


1.       Let request be fetchParams’ [request](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy").

2.       Let response be the result of executing [potentially override response for a request](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy") on request.

3.       If response is non-null, then return response.

4.       Switch on type and run the associated step:  
"`scheme fetch`"                  Set response be the result of running [scheme fetch](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#enqueue-the-following-steps "Referrer Policy") given   fetchParams.   
"`HTTP fetch`"                  Set response be the result of running [HTTP fetch](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy") given   fetchParams and makeCORSPreflight.  

5.       Return response.


The *potentially override response for a request* algorithm takes a [request](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer
Policy")request, and returns either a [response](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") or null. Its behavior is[implementation-defined](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy"), allowing user agents to intervene on the [request](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") byreturning a response directly, or allowing the request to proceed by returning null.

By default, the algorithm has the following trivial implementation:


1.       Return null.


User agents will generally override this default implementation with a somewhat more
complex set of behaviors. For example, a user agent might decide that its users'
safety is best preserved by generally blocking requests to \`https://unsafe.example/\`,
while synthesizing a shim for the widely-used resource \`https://unsafe.example/widget.js\`
to avoid breakage. That implementation might look like the following:


1.        If request’s [current url](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy")’s [host](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy")’s  [registrable domain](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy") is "`unsafe.example`":  


1.          If request’s [current url](#local-scheme "Referrer Policy")’s [path](#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy") is   « "`widget.js`" »:    


1.            Let body be [*insert a byte sequence representing the shimmed      content here*].     

2.            Return a new [response](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") with the following properties:      
[type](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")                   "`cors`"                   [status](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")                   200            ...                   ...                   [body](#fetch-scheme "HTML Standard")                   The result of getting body [as a body](#http "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").                          


-          Return a [network error](#concept-fetch "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").   


-        Return null.


### 4.3. Scheme fetch


To *scheme fetch*, given a[fetch params](#http-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
fetchParams:


1.       If fetchParams is [canceled](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."), then return the [appropriate network error](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") for fetchParams.

2.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").

3.       Switch on request’s [current URL](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [scheme](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") and run  the associated steps:  
"`about`"                  If request’s [current URL](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [path](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is the string    "`blank`", then return a new [response](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") whose    [status message](#http-tab-or-space-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is \``OK`\`, [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is «    (\``Content-Type`\`, \``text/html;charset=utf-8`\`) », and    [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is the empty byte sequence [as a body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").    

	[URLs](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") such as "`about:config`" are handled during    [navigation](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") and result in a [network error](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") in the context of    [fetching](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").   
"`blob`"                 

1.           Let blobURLEntry be request’s [current URL](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s     [blob URL entry](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").     

2.           If request’s [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is not \``GET`\` or      blobURLEntry is null, then return a [network error](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."). [[FILEAPI]](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")      

	The \``GET`\` [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") restriction serves no useful purpose      other than being interoperable.     

3.           Let requestEnvironment be the result of     [determining the environment](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") given request.     

4.           Let isTopLevelSelfFetch be false.     

5.           If request’s [client](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is non-null:      


1.             Let global be request’s [client](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s       [global object](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").       

2.             If all of the following conditions are true:        
3.               global is a `[Window](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object;         

4.               global’s [navigable](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is not null;         

5.               global’s [navigable](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [parent](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")         is null; and         

6.               requestEnvironment’s [creation URL](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")         [equals](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") request’s [current URL](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css),        

then set isTopLevelSelfFetch to true.      


-           Let stringOrEnvironment be the result of these steps:      


1.             If request’s [destination](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle") is "`document`",       then return "`top-level-navigation`".       

2.             If isTopLevelSelfFetch is true, then return       "`top-level-self-fetch`".       

3.             Return requestEnvironment.      


-           Let blob be the result of [obtaining a blob object](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given     blobURLEntry and stringOrEnvironment.     

-           If blob is not a `[Blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, then return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

-           Let response be a new [response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

-           Let fullLength be blob’s `[size](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.     

-           Let serializedFullLength be fullLength,     [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

-           Let type be blob’s `[type](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.     

-           If request’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")      [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Range`\`:      


1.             Let bodyWithType be the result of [safely extracting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       blob.       

2.             Set response’s [status message](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to \``OK`\`.       

3.             Set response’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to bodyWithType’s       [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

4.             Set response’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to «       (\``Content-Length`\`, serializedFullLength),       (\``Content-Type`\`, type) ».      


-           Otherwise:      


1.             Set response’s [range-requested flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

2.             Let rangeHeader be the result of [getting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       \``Range`\` from request’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

3.             Let rangeValue be the result of [parsing a single range header value](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       given rangeHeader and true.       

4.             If rangeValue is failure, then return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

5.             Let (rangeStart, rangeEnd) be rangeValue.       

6.             If rangeStart is null:        


1.               Set rangeStart to fullLength − rangeEnd.         

2.               Set rangeEnd to rangeStart + rangeEnd − 1.        


-             Otherwise:        


1.               If rangeStart is greater than or equal to fullLength, then         return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         

2.               If rangeEnd is null or rangeEnd is greater than or equal to         fullLength, then set rangeEnd to fullLength − 1.        


-             Let slicedBlob be the result of invoking [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") given        blob, rangeStart, rangeEnd + 1, and type.        

	A range header denotes an inclusive byte range, while the [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")        algorithm input range does not. To use the [slice blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") algorithm, we have to increment        rangeEnd.       

-             Let slicedBodyWithType be the result of       [safely extracting](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") slicedBlob.       

-             Set response’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to slicedBodyWithType’s       [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

-             Let serializedSlicedLength be slicedBlob’s `[size](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`,       [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [isomorphic encoded](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

-             Let contentRange be the result of invoking [build a content range](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")       given rangeStart, rangeEnd, and fullLength.       

-             Set response’s [status](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to 206.       

-             Set response’s [status message](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to       \``Partial Content`\`.       

-             Set response’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to «       (\``Content-Length`\`, serializedSlicedLength),       (\``Content-Type`\`, type), (\``Content-Range`\`,       contentRange) ».      


-           Return response.    


"`data`"                 

1.           Let dataURLStruct be the result of running the     [`data:` URL processor](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") on request’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

2.           If dataURLStruct is failure, then return a [network error](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

3.           Let mimeType be dataURLStruct’s     [MIME type](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [serialized](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

4.           Return a new [response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [status message](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") is     \``OK`\`, [header list](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle") is « (\``Content-Type`\`,     mimeType) », and [body](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") is dataURLStruct’s     [body](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key") [as a body](https://whatwg.org/chat "You can also press the 'y' key").    


"`file`"                  
For now, unfortunate as it is, `file:` [URLs](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key") are left as an exercise    for the reader.    

When in doubt, return a [network error](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese").   
[HTTP(S) scheme](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese")           

Return the result of running [HTTP fetch](ja "Simplified Chinese") given fetchParams.


-       
Return a [network error](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean").


To *determine the environment*, given a [request](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")request:


1.       If request’s [reserved client](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then return request’s [reserved client](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3").

2.       If request’s [client](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then return request’s [client](#methods "Content Security Policy Level 3").

3.       Return null.


### 4.4. HTTP fetch


To *HTTP fetch*, given a [fetch params](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3")fetchParams
and an optional boolean makeCORSPreflight (default false), runthese steps:


1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3").

2.       Let response and internalResponse be null.

3.       If request’s [service-workers mode](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`all`", then:  


1.         Let requestForServiceWorker be a [clone](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3") of   request.   

2.         If requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then:    


1.           Let transformStream be a new `[TransformStream](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.     

2.           Let transformAlgorithm given chunk be these steps:      


1.             If fetchParams is [canceled](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then abort these       steps.       

2.             If chunk is not a `[Uint8Array](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, then       [terminate](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3") fetchParams’s       [controller](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level 3").       

3.             Otherwise, [enqueue](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") chunk in       transformStream. The user agent may split the chunk into       [implementation-defined](#http-requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") practical sizes and [enqueue](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level 3") each of       them. The user agent also may concatenate the chunks into an [implementation-defined](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3")       practical size and [enqueue](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") it.      


-           [Set up](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") transformStream with     [*transformAlgorithm*](#content-type-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") set to     transformAlgorithm.     

-           Set requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     the result of requestForServiceWorker’s [body](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")     [piped through](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") transformStream.    


-         Let serviceWorkerStartTime be the [coarsened shared current time](#main-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")   given fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3").   

-         Let fetchResponse be the result of invoking [handle fetch](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") for   requestForServiceWorker, with fetchParams’s   [controller](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") and fetchParams’s   [cross-origin isolated capability](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3"). [[HTML]](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") [[SW]](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3")   

-         If fetchResponse is a [response](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3"):    


1.           Set response to fetchResponse.     

2.           Set fetchParams’s [timing info](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s     [final service worker start time](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") to     serviceWorkerStartTime.     

3.           Set fetchParams’s [timing info](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s     [service worker timing info](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3") to response’s     [service worker timing info](#response-class "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

4.           If request’s [body](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null, then     [cancel](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") request’s [body](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3") with undefined.     

5.           Set internalResponse to response, if response is not a     [filtered response](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3"); otherwise to response’s     [internal response](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3").     

6.           Run the [WebDriver BiDi response started](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3") steps with     request and response.     

7.           If one of the following is true      
8.             response’s [type](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`error`"       

9.             request’s [mode](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`same-origin`" and       response’s [type](#acknowledgments "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`cors`"       

10.             request’s [mode](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`no-cors`" and       response’s [type](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`opaque`"       

11. request’s [redirect mode](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`manual`" and       response’s [type](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") is "`opaqueredirect`"                  
12. request’s [redirect mode](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not "`follow`" and       response’s [URL list](#idl-index "Content Security Policy Level 3") has more than one item                
then return a [network error](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3").    


-         Otherwise, if fetchResponse is a [service worker timing info](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3"),   then set fetchParams’s [timing info](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Fetch Metadata Request Headers")’s   [service worker timing info](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly") to fetchResponse.  


-       
If response is null, then:  


1.         If makeCORSPreflight is true and one of these conditions is true:    
2.           There is no [method cache entry match](#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy") for request’s     [method](#http-origin "The Web Origin Concept") using request, and either request’s     [method](#biblio-origin "The Web Origin Concept") is not a [CORS-safelisted method](#preface "Infra Standard") or request’s     [use-CORS-preflight flag](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") is set.     

3. There is at least one [item](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") in the [CORS-unsafe request-header names](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")     with request’s [header list](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") for which there is no     [header-name cache entry match](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard") using request.            

Then:    


1.           Let preflightResponse be the result of running [CORS-preflight fetch](#infrastructure "Infra Standard")     given request.     

2.           If preflightResponse is a [network error](#infrastructure "Infra Standard"), then return     preflightResponse.    


This step checks the [CORS-preflight cache](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF") and if there is no suitable entry    it performs a [CORS-preflight fetch](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF") which, if successful, populates the cache. The purpose    of the [CORS-preflight fetch](#biblio-encoding "Encoding Standard") is to ensure the [fetched](#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics") resource is    familiar with the [CORS protocol](#biblio-mimesniff "MIME Sniffing Standard"). The cache is there to minimize the number of    [CORS-preflight fetches](#biblio-url "URL Standard").   

-         
If request’s [redirect mode](#biblio-websockets "WebSockets Standard") is "`follow`",
then set    request’s [service-workers mode](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF") to
"`none`".    

Redirects coming from the network (as opposed to from a service worker) are not to
be exposed to a service worker.   

-         
Set response and internalResponse to the result of running   [HTTP-network-or-cache fetch](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") given
fetchParams.   

-         
If request’s [response tainting](#biblio-cookies "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") is
"`cors`" and a    [CORS check](#biblio-tls "The Transport Layer Security (TLS) Protocol Version 1.3") for
request and response returns failure, then return a    [network error](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing").    

As the [CORS check](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") is not to be applied to [responses](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing") whose    [status](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing") is 304 or 407, or [responses](#concept-request "Resource Timing") from a service worker for    that matter, it is applied here.   

-         
If the [TAO check](#concept-request "Resource Timing") for request and response returns
failure,   then set request’s [timing allow failed flag](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").  


-       
If either request’s [response tainting](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") or
response’s  [type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is
"`opaque`", and the  [cross-origin resource policy check](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") with
request’s [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"),
request’s [client](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"),
request’s  [destination](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"), and
internalResponse returns **blocked**, then  return a [network error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").  

The [cross-origin resource policy check](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") runs for responses coming from the  network and responses coming from the service worker. This is different from the  [CORS check](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"), as
request’s [client](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") and the service worker can  have different embedder policies.

-       
If internalResponse’s [status](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Resource Timing") is a [redirect status](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"):  


1.         If request is a [navigation request](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then   [append to a request’s navigation timing allow values list](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") given request and   internalResponse.   

2.         If internalResponse’s [status](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is not 303, request’s    [body](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") is non-null, and the [connection](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") uses HTTP/2, then user agents    may, and are even encouraged to, transmit an `RST_STREAM` frame.    

	303 is excluded as certain communities ascribe special status to it.   

3.         Switch on request’s    [redirect mode](#concept-response "Resource Timing"):    
"`error`"                       

1.             Set response to a [network error](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing").


"`manual`"                       

1.             If request’s [mode](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing") is "`navigate`", then set       fetchParams’s [controller](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s       [next manual redirect steps](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") to run [HTTP-redirect fetch](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")       given fetchParams and response.       

2.             Otherwise, set response to an [opaque-redirect filtered response](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")       whose [internal response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is internalResponse.      


"`follow`"                       

1. Run the [WebDriver BiDi response completed](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") steps with request and       response.                  
2.             Set response to the result of running [HTTP-redirect fetch](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") given       fetchParams and response.      


-       
Return response. Typically internalResponse’s [body](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s [stream](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is still being enqueued to after returning.


### 4.5. HTTP-redirect fetch


To *HTTP-redirect fetch*, given a[fetch params](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") fetchParams
and a [response](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")
response,run these steps:


1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing").

2.       Let internalResponse be response, if response is not a [filtered response](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing"); otherwise response’s [internal response](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

3.       Let locationURL be internalResponse’s [location URL](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") given request’s [current URL](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s [fragment](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

4.       If locationURL is null, then return response.

5.       If locationURL is failure, then return a [network error](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing").

6.       If locationURL’s [scheme](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing") is not an [HTTP(S) scheme](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then return a [network error](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

7.       If request’s [redirect count](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is 20, then return a [network error](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

8.       Increase request’s [redirect count](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing") by 1.

9.       If request’s [mode](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing") is "`cors`", locationURL [includes credentials](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing"), and request’s [origin](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is not [same origin](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") with locationURL’s [origin](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then return a [network error](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing").

10.       If request’s [response tainting](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") is "`cors`" and  locationURL [includes credentials](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing"), then return a [network error](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing").  

	This catches a cross-origin resource redirecting to a same-origin URL.

11.       If internalResponse’s [status](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") is not 303, request’s [body](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") is non-null, and request’s [body](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing")’s [source](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing") is null, then return a [network error](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing").

12.       If one of the following is true  
13.         internalResponse’s [status](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") is 301 or 302 and   request’s [method](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") is \``POST`\`   

14.         internalResponse’s [status](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm "Resource Timing") is 303 and request’s   [method](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") is not \``GET`\` or \``HEAD`\`  


then:  


1.         Set request’s [method](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException "Resource Timing") to \``GET`\` and   request’s [body](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing") to null.   

2.         [For each](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing") headerName of [request-body-header name](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"),   [delete](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") headerName from request’s   [header list](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").  


-       
If request’s [current URL](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s [origin](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is not  [same origin](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") with
locationURL’s [origin](#fetch-params "Resource Timing"), then  [for each](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing")
headerName of [CORS non-wildcard request-header name](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing"),  [delete](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing")
headerName from request’s  [header list](#fetch-params "Resource Timing").  

I.e., the moment another origin is seen after the initial request, the  \``Authorization`\` header
is removed.

-       
If request’s [body](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") is non-null, then set request’s
[body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") to the [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") of the result of  [safely extracting](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")
request’s [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")’s  [source](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing").  

request’s [body](#biblio-resource-timing "Resource Timing")’s [source](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing")’s nullity has  already been checked.

-       
Let timingInfo be fetchParams’s [timing info](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       
Set timingInfo’s [redirect end time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") and [post-redirect start time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to the [coarsened shared current time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") given
fetchParams’s [cross-origin isolated capability](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       
If timingInfo’s [redirect start time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") is 0, then set
timingInfo’s [redirect start time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to
timingInfo’s [start time](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       
[Append](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") locationURL
to request’s [URL list](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").

-       
Invoke [set request’s referrer policy on redirect](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") on
request and internalResponse. [[REFERRER]](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")

-       
Let recursive be true.

-       
If request’s [redirect mode](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") is
"`manual`", then:  


1.         [Assert](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing"): request’s [mode](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") is   "`navigate`".   

2.         Set recursive to false.  


-       
Return the result of running [main fetch](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") given
fetchParams and  recursive.  

This has to invoke [main fetch](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") to get
request’s  [response tainting](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing") correct.


### 4.6. HTTP-network-or-cache fetch


To *HTTP-network-or-cache fetch*, given a[fetch params](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")
fetchParams, an optional booleanisAuthenticationFetch (default false), and an optional
booleanisNewConnectionFetch (default false), run these steps:

Some implementations might support caching of partial content, as per*HTTP Caching*.
However, this is not widely supported by browser caches.[[HTTP-CACHING]](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")


1.       Let request be fetchParams’s [request](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing").

2.       Let httpFetchParams be null.

3.       Let httpRequest be null.

4.       Let response be null.

5.       Let storedResponse be null.

6.       Let httpCache be null.

7.       Let the revalidatingFlag be unset.

8.       Run these steps, but [abort when](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") fetchParams is  [canceled](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy"):  


1.         If request’s [traversable for user prompts](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is    "`no-traversable`" and request’s [redirect mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is    "`error`", then set httpFetchParams to fetchParams and    httpRequest to request.   

2.         Otherwise:    


1.           Set httpRequest to a [clone](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") of request.      

	Implementations are encouraged to avoid teeing request’s      [body](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy")’s [stream](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") when request’s      [body](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy")’s [source](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy") is null as only a single body is needed in      that case. E.g., when request’s [body](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy")’s [source](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy")      is null, redirects and authentication will end up failing the fetch.     

2.           Set httpFetchParams to a copy of fetchParams.     

3.           Set httpFetchParams’s [request](#fetch-timing-info-render-blocking "Referrer Policy") to     httpRequest.    


If user prompts or redirects are possible, then the user agent might need to    re-send the request with a new set of headers after the user answers the prompt or the redirect    location is determined. At that time, the original request body might have been partially sent    already, so we need to clone the request (including the body) beforehand so that we have a    spare copy available.   

-         Let includeCredentials be true if one of    
- request’s [credentials mode](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy") is     "`include`"              
- request’s  [credentials mode](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy") is     "`same-origin`" and request’s     [response tainting](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#enqueue-the-following-steps "Referrer Policy") is "`basic`"            
is true; otherwise false.   

-         If [Cross-Origin-Embedder-Policy allows credentials](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy") with request returns   false, then set includeCredentials to false.   

-         Let contentLength be httpRequest’s [body](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object "Referrer Policy")’s   [length](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object "Referrer Policy"), if httpRequest’s [body](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") is non-null;   otherwise null.   

-         Let contentLengthHeaderValue be null.   

-         If httpRequest’s [body](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") is null and httpRequest’s   [method](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") is \``POST`\` or \``PUT`\`, then set   contentLengthHeaderValue to \``0`\`.   

-         If contentLength is non-null, then set  contentLengthHeaderValue to   contentLength, [serialized](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") and   [isomorphic encoded](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy").   

-         If contentLengthHeaderValue is non-null, then [append](#url "Referrer Policy")   (\``Content-Length`\`, contentLengthHeaderValue) to httpRequest’s   [header list](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy").   

-         If contentLength is non-null and httpRequest’s    [keepalive](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy") is true, then:    


1.           Let inflightKeepaliveBytes be 0.     

2.           Let group be httpRequest’s [client](#local-scheme "Referrer Policy")’s     [fetch group](#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy").     

3.           Let inflightRecords be the set of [fetch records](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") in     group whose [request](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")’s [keepalive](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") is true     and [done flag](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") is unset.     

4.           [For each](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") fetchRecord of inflightRecords:      


1.             Let inflightRequest be fetchRecord’s       [request](#http "HTML Standard").       

2.             Increment inflightKeepaliveBytes by inflightRequest’s       [body](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [length](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").      


-           If the sum of contentLength and inflightKeepaliveBytes is greater     than 64 kibibytes, then return a [network error](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").    


The above limit ensures that requests that are allowed to outlive the    [environment settings object](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") and contain a body, have a bounded size and are not allowed    to stay alive indefinitely.   

-         If httpRequest’s [referrer](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is a [URL](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."), then:    


1.           Let referrerValue be httpRequest’s [referrer](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."),     [serialized](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") and [isomorphic encoded](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").     

2.           [Append](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") (\``Referer`\`, referrerValue) to     httpRequest’s [header list](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").    


-         [Append a request \``Origin`\` header](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") for httpRequest.   

-         [Append the Fetch metadata headers for httpRequest](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").   [[FETCH-METADATA]](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")   

-         If httpRequest’s [initiator](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`prefetch`", then   [set a structured field value](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") given (\`[`Sec-Purpose`](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")\`,   the [token](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") `prefetch`) in   httpRequest’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").   

-         If httpRequest’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") [does not contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") \``User-Agent`\`, then user agents should:    


1.           Let userAgent be httpRequest’s [client](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s     [environment default \``User-Agent`\` value](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").     

2.           [Append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") (\``User-Agent`\`, userAgent) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").    


-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`default`" and   httpRequest’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") [contains](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")   \``If-Modified-Since`\`,   \``If-None-Match`\`,   \``If-Unmodified-Since`\`,   \``If-Match`\`, or   \``If-Range`\`, then set httpRequest’s   [cache mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") to "`no-store`".   

-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`no-cache`",   httpRequest’s [prevent no-cache cache-control header modification flag](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")   is unset, and httpRequest’s [header list](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")   [does not contain](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") \``Cache-Control`\`, then   [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") (\``Cache-Control`\`, \``max-age=0`\`) to   httpRequest’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").   

-         If httpRequest’s [cache mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`no-store`" or    "`reload`", then:    


1.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css)     [does not contain](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css) \``Pragma`\`, then     [append](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css "subtitle") (\``Pragma`\`, \``no-cache`\`) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://resources.whatwg.org/spec.css "subtitle").     

2.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")     [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Cache-Control`\`, then     [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\``Cache-Control`\`, \``no-cache`\`) to     httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    


-         If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [contains](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")    \``Range`\`, then [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") (\``Accept-Encoding`\`,    \``identity`\`) to httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    


This avoids a failure when [handling content codings](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with     a part of an encoded [response](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").     

	Additionally,     [many servers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")     mistakenly ignore \``Range`\` headers if a non-identity encoding is accepted.    

-         Modify httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") per HTTP. Do not    [append](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a given [header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") if httpRequest’s    [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [contains](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") that [header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s    [name](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    

	It would be great if we could make this more normative somehow. At this point    [headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") such as    \``Accept-Encoding`\`,    \``Connection`\`,    \``DNT`\`, and    \``Host`\`,    are to be [appended](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") if necessary.    

	\``Accept`\`,    \``Accept-Charset`\`, and    \``Accept-Language`\` must not be included at this point.    

	\``Accept`\` and \``Accept-Language`\` are already included    (unless [`fetch()`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is used, which does not include the latter by    default), and \``Accept-Charset`\` is a waste of bytes. See    [HTTP header layer division](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for more details.   

-         If includeCredentials is true, then:    


1.           [Append a request \``Cookie`\` header](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for httpRequest.     

2.           If httpRequest’s [header list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")      [does not contain](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") \``Authorization`\`, then:      


1.             Let authorizationValue be null.       

2.             If there’s an [authentication entry](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for httpRequest and either       httpRequest’s [use-URL-credentials flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is unset or       httpRequest’s [current URL](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") does not [include credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"),       then set authorizationValue to [authentication entry](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").       

3.
