"Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[reserved deferred-fetch quota](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") to 0.
    
This is called when a [document](#cross-origin-resource-policy-header "Content Security Policy Level 3") is created. It ensures that same-originnested documents don’t reserve quota, as they anyway share their parent quota. It can only be calledupon document creation, as the [origin](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3") of the [document](#main-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") is only knownafter redirects are handled.
      

    
To get the *deferred-fetch control document* of a [document](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level
3")document:
   

     1.       If document’ [node navigable](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [container document](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null or a [document](#cors-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") whose [origin](#tao-check "Content Security Policy Level 3") is not [same origin](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") with document, then return document; otherwise, return the [deferred-fetch control document](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3") given document’s [node navigable](#headers-class "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [container document](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3").
    

      
## 5. Fetch API
   
The [`fetch()`](#response-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") method is relatively low-level API for[fetching](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") resources. It covers slightly more ground than `[XMLHttpRequest](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level
3")`,although it is currently lacking when it comes to request progression (not 
response progression).
   

         
The [`fetch()`](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3") method makes it quite straightforward to [fetch](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") a resource and extract its contents as a `[Blob](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level
3")`: 

```secno
fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
.then(res => res.blob()).then(playBlob)
```
    
If you just care to log a particular response header: 

```toc
fetch("/", {method:"HEAD"})
.then(res => log(res.headers.get("strict-transport-security")))
```
    
If you want to check a particular response header and then process the response of 
a cross-origin resource: 

```secno
fetch("https://pk.example/berlin-calling.json", {mode:"cors"})
.then(res => {
if(res.headers.get("content-type") &&
res.headers.get("content-type").toLowerCase().indexOf("application/json") >= 0) {
return res.json()
} else {
throw new TypeError()
}
}).then(processJSON)
```
    
If you want to work with URL query parameters: 

```heading no-num settled short
var url = new URL("https://geo.example.org/api"),
params = {lat:35.696233, long:139.570431}
Object.keys(params).forEach(key => url.searchParams.append(key, params[key]))
fetch(url).then(/* … */)
```
    
If you want to receive the body data progressively: 

```heading settled short
function consume(reader) {
var total = 0
return pump()
function pump() {
return reader.read().then(({done, value}) => {
if (done) {
return
}
total += value.byteLength
log(`received ${value.byteLength} bytes (${total} bytes in total)`)
return pump()
})
}
}

fetch("/music/pk/altes-kamuffel.flac")
.then(res => consume(res.body.getReader()))
.then(() => log("consumed the entire body without keeping the whole thing in memory!"))
.catch(e => log("something went wrong: " + e))
```
      
### 5.1. Headers class

```typedef ([sequence](#infrastructure "Infra Standard")<[sequence](#infrastructure "Infra
Standard")<[ByteString](#infrastructure "Infra Standard")>> or [record](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications:
ABNF")<[ByteString](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF"), [ByteString](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF")>) *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
HeadersInit*;[[Exposed](#biblio-mimesniff "MIME Sniffing Standard")=(Window,Worker)]interface
*dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
Headers* {  [constructor](#biblio-webtransport "WebTransport")(optional [HeadersInit](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
init*);  [undefined](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") [append](#biblio-cookies "Cookies: HTTP State Management
Mechanism")([ByteString](#biblio-tls "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
name*, [ByteString](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
value*);  [undefined](#concept-request "Resource Timing") [delete](#concept-request "Resource
Timing")([ByteString](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
name*);  [ByteString](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")?
[get](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource
Timing")([ByteString](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
name*);  [sequence](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource
Timing")<[ByteString](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")>
[getSetCookie](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")();
[boolean](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")
[has](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource
Timing")([ByteString](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
name*);  [undefined](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") [set](#fetch-controller "Resource
Timing")([ByteString](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
name*, [ByteString](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
value*);  [iterable](#concept-response "Resource Timing")<[ByteString](#concept-response "Resource Timing"),
[ByteString](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")>;};```

A `[Headers](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")` object has an associated*header
list* (a[header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")), which is initially empty.
Thiscan be a pointer to the [header list](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") of something else, e.g.,of a [request](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") as demonstrated by `[Request](#fetch-timing-info "Resource
Timing")`objects.

A `[Headers](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")` object also has an associated*guard*,
which is a *headers guard*. A[headers guard](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is
"`immutable`", "`request`","`request-no-cors`", "`response`" or "`none`".

---
`headers = new [Headers](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")([init])`

Creates a new `[Headers](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")`
object. init can be used to fill its internal header list,  as per the example below.


```dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
const meta = { "Content-Type": "text/xml", "Breaking-Bad": "<3" };
new Headers(meta);

// The above is equivalent to
const meta2 = [
  [ "Content-Type", "text/xml" ],
  [ "Breaking-Bad", "<3" ]
];
new Headers(meta2);
```
`headers . [append](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")(name, value)`          
Appends a header to headers.
`headers . [delete](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing")(name)`          
Removes a header from headers.
`headers . [get](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing")(name)`          
Returns as a string the values of all headers whose name is name, separated by a
comma and a space.
`headers . [getSetCookie](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")()`          
Returns a list of the values for all headers whose name is \``Set-Cookie`\`.
`headers . [has](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")(name)`          
Returns whether there is a header whose name is name.
`headers . [set](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing")(name, value)`          
Replaces the value of the first header whose name is name with value and removes
any remaining headers whose name is name.
`for(const [name, value] of headers)`          
headers can be iterated over.

---


To *validate* a  [header](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") (name, value) fora
`[Headers](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing")` object headers:


1.       If name is not a [header name](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing") or value is not a [header value](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing"), then [throw](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")`.

2.       If headers’s [guard](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is "`immutable`", then [throw](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing")`.

3.       If headers’s [guard](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") is "`request`" and (name, value) is a [forbidden request-header](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing"), then return false.

4.       If headers’s [guard](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") is "`response`" and name is a [forbidden response-header name](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing"), then return false.

5.       Return true.


Steps for "`request-no-cors`" are not shared as you cannot have a fakevalue (for `[delete()](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource
Timing")`) that always succeeds in [CORS-safelisted request-header](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing").


To *append* a [header](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource
Timing")(name, value) to a `[Headers](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")`
object headers, run these steps:


1.       [Normalize](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm "Resource Timing") value.

2.       If [validating](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") (name, value) for headers returns false, then return.

3.       If headers’s [guard](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException "Resource Timing") is "`request-no-cors`":  


1.         Let temporaryValue be the result of [getting](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing")   name from headers’s [header list](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").   

2.         If temporaryValue is null, then set temporaryValue to   value.   

3.         Otherwise, set temporaryValue to temporaryValue, followed by   0x2C 0x20, followed by value.   

4.         If (name, temporaryValue) is not a   [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then return.  


-       [Append](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") (name, value) to headers’s [header list](#fetch-params "Resource Timing").

-       If headers’s [guard](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") from headers.


To *fill* a `[Headers](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing")` objectheaders with a
given object object, run these steps:


1.       If object is a [sequence](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing"), then [for each](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") header of  object:  


1.         If header’s [size](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") is not 2, then [throw](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")`.   

2.         [Append](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing") (header[0], header[1]) to   headers.  


-       Otherwise, object is a [record](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"), then [for each](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") key → value of object, [append](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") (key, value) to headers.


To*remove privileged no-CORS request-headers*from a `[Headers](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")`
object (headers), run these steps:


1.       [For each](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") headerName of [privileged no-CORS request-header names](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"):


1.         [Delete](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") headerName from headers’s  [header list](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing").


This is called when headers are modified by unprivileged code.


The*`new Headers(init)`*constructor steps are:


1.       Set [this](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s [guard](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") to "`none`".

2.       If init is given, then [fill](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") [this](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") with init.


The *`append(name, value)`*method steps are to [append](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing") (name,
value) to [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing").


The *`delete(name)`* method steps are:


1.       If [validating](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") (name, \`\`) for [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") returns false, then  return.  

	Passing a dummy [header value](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") ought not to have any negative repercussions.

2.       If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")’s [guard](#biblio-resource-timing "Resource Timing") is "`request-no-cors`", name is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header name](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing"), and name is not a [privileged no-CORS request-header name](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy"), then return.

3.       If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") [does not contain](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") name, then return.

4.       [Delete](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") name from [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy").

5.       If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [guard](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") from [this](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy").


The *`get(name)`* method steps are:


1.       If name is not a [header name](#fetch-timing-info-post-redirect-start-time "Referrer Policy"), then [throw](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy") a `[TypeError](#fetch-timing-info-end-time "Referrer Policy")`.

2.       Return the result of [getting](#fetch-timing-info-end-time "Referrer Policy") name from [this](#fetch-timing-info-render-blocking "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](#fetch-timing-info-render-blocking "Referrer Policy").


The *`getSetCookie()`* method steps are:


1.       If [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy") [does not contain](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy") \``Set-Cookie`\`, then return « ».

2.       Return the [values](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#enqueue-the-following-steps "Referrer Policy") of all [headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#enqueue-the-following-steps "Referrer Policy") in [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy") whose [name](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#networking-task-source "Referrer Policy") is a [byte-case-insensitive](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#networking-task-source "Referrer Policy") match for \``Set-Cookie`\`, in order.


The *`has(name)`* method steps are:


1.       If name is not a [header name](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy"), then [throw](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") a `[TypeError](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy")`.

2.       Return true if [this](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") [contains](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") name; otherwise false.


The *`set(name, value)`*method steps are:


1.       [Normalize](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy") value.

2.       If [validating](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy") (name, value) for [this](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy") returns false, then return.

3.       If [this](#local-scheme "Referrer Policy")’s [guard](#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy") is "`request-no-cors`" and (name, value) is not a [no-CORS-safelisted request-header](#http-scheme "Referrer Policy"), then return.

4.       [Set](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") (name, value) in [this](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")’s [header list](#http-scheme "HTML Standard").

5.       If [this](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")’s [guard](#fetch-scheme "HTML Standard") is "`request-no-cors`", then [remove privileged no-CORS request-headers](#biblio-html "HTML Standard") from [this](#http "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").


The [value pairs to iterate over](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") are the return value of running[sort and combine](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") with [this](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [header list](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").

### 5.2. BodyInit unions

```typedef ([Blob](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
or [BufferSource](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
or [FormData](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
or [URLSearchParams](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
or [USVString](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")) *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
XMLHttpRequestBodyInit*;typedef ([ReadableStream](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")
or [XMLHttpRequestBodyInit](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")) *algorithm
BodyInit*;```
   
To *safely extract* a [body with type](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") from a[byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") or `[BodyInit](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` 
object object, run these steps:
  

    1.      If object is a `[ReadableStream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object, then:  
    

      1.        [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."): object is neither [disturbed](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") nor   [locked](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").  
     

    
-      Return the result of [extracting](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") object.
   

   
The [safely extract](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") operation is a subset of the[extract](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") operation that is guaranteed to not throw an exception.
   
To *extract* a[body with type](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") from a [byte sequence](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") or `[BodyInit](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` 
objectobject, with an optional boolean*keepalive* (default false), run thesesteps:
  

    1.      Let stream be null. 
    
2.      If object is a `[ReadableStream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object, then set stream to object. 
    
3.      Otherwise, if object is a `[Blob](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object, set stream to the result of running object’s [get stream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."). 
    
4.      Otherwise, set stream to a [new](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") `[ReadableStream](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object, and [set up](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") stream with byte reading support. 
    
5.      [Assert](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."): stream is a `[ReadableStream](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")` object. 
    
6.      Let action be null. 
    
7.      Let source be null. 
    
8.      Let length be null. 
    
9.      Let type be null. 
    
10.      Switch on object:  
           `[Blob](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css)`                Set source to object.    
       
	Set length to object’s `[size](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle")`.    
       
	If object’s `[type](https://resources.whatwg.org/logo-fetch.svg "subtitle")`    attribute is not the empty byte sequence, set type to its value.   
      [byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")                Set source to object.   
      `[BufferSource](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set source to a [copy of the bytes](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")   held by object.   
      `[FormData](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set action to this step: run the    [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), with object’s    [entry list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and [UTF-8](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
       
	Set source to object.    
       
	Set length to unclear, see    [html/6424](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") for improving this.    
       
	Set type to \``multipart/form-data; boundary=`\`, followed by the    [`multipart/form-data` boundary string](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") generated by the    [`multipart/form-data` encoding algorithm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").   
      `[URLSearchParams](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                Set source to the result of running the    [`application/x-www-form-urlencoded` serializer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") with    object’s [list](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").    
       
	Set type to    \``application/x-www-form-urlencoded;charset=UTF-8`\`.   
      [scalar value string](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")                Set source to the [UTF-8 encoding](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") of object.    
       
	Set type to \``text/plain;charset=UTF-8`\`.   
      `[ReadableStream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`                If keepalive is true, then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.    
       
	If object is [disturbed](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") or    [locked](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.  
         
11.      If source is a [byte sequence](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then set action to a step that returns source and length to source’s [length](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
    
12.      If action is non-null, then run these steps [in parallel](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"):  
    

      1.        Run action.    
       
	Whenever one or more bytes are available and stream is not    [errored](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), [enqueue](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") the result of    [creating](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[Uint8Array](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` from the available bytes into    stream.    
       
	When running action is done, [close](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") stream.  
     

    
-      Let body be a [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") whose [stream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is stream, [source](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is source, and [length](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is length. 
    
-      Return (body, type).
   

   
### 5.3. Body mixin

```interface mixin *h-entry status-LS
Body* {  readonly attribute [ReadableStream](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")? [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle");
readonly attribute [boolean](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [bodyUsed](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle");
[[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")<[ArrayBuffer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")> [arrayBuffer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")();
[[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")<[Blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")>
[blob](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")();  [[NewObject](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")] [Promise](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")<[Uint8Array](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")> [bytes](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")();
[[NewObject](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle")] [Promise](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key")<[FormData](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")> [formData](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key")();
[[NewObject](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key")] [Promise](/commit-snapshots/394d20d144ed1401c2c0e02c35bc3608cb2a2269/ "You can also press the 'y'
key")<[any](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "Japanese")> [json](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")();
[[NewObject](https://triple-underscore.github.io/Fetch-ja.html "Japanese")] [Promise](https://htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Simplified
Chinese")<[USVString](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean")> [text](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")();
[[NewObject](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")] [ReadableStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")
[textStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")();};```

Formats you would not want a network layer to be dependent upon, such asHTML, will
likely not be exposed here. Rather, an HTML parser API might accept a stream indue
course.

Objects including the `[Body](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3")` interface mixin
have an associated*body* (null or a [body](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3")).

An object including the `[Body](#statuses "Content Security Policy Level 3")` interface
mixin is said to be*unusable* if its [body](#responses "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null and its[body](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [stream](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [disturbed](#authentication-entries "Content Security Policy Level 3") or[locked](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3").

---
`requestOrResponse . [body](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3")`          
Returns requestOrResponse’s body as `[ReadableStream](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level
3")`.
`requestOrResponse . [bodyUsed](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")`          
Returns whether requestOrResponse’s body has been read from.
`requestOrResponse . [arrayBuffer](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[ArrayBuffer](#cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level
3")`.
`requestOrResponse . [blob](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[Blob](#origin-header "Content Security Policy Level
3")`.
`requestOrResponse . [bytes](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[Uint8Array](#general "Content Security Policy Level
3")`.
`requestOrResponse . [formData](#http-responses "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as `[FormData](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level
3")`.
`requestOrResponse . [json](#cors-protocol-examples "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body parsed as JSON.
`requestOrResponse . [text](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`          
Returns a promise fulfilled with requestOrResponse’s body as string.
`requestOrResponse . [textStream](#x-content-type-options-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`       

Returns a `[ReadableStream](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
object with requestOrResponse’s body as string chunks.

---


To *get the MIME type*, given a `[Request](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
or`[Response](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object requestOrResponse:


1.       Let headers be null.

2.       If requestOrResponse is a `[Request](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, then set headers to requestOrResponse’s [request](#http-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [header list](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3").

3.       Otherwise, set headers to requestOrResponse’s [response](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [header list](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3").

4.       Let mimeType be the result of [extracting a MIME type](#cors-preflight-cache "Content Security Policy Level 3") from headers.

5.       If mimeType is failure, then return null.

6.       Return mimeType.


The *`body`* getter steps are to return null if[this](#fetch-api "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#headers-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null; otherwise [this](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#bodyinit-unions "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[stream](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3").


The *`bodyUsed`* getter steps are to return true if[this](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") is non-null and [this](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [body](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s[stream](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3") is [disturbed](#atomic-http-redirect-handling "Content Security Policy Level 3"); otherwise false.


The *consume body*algorithm, given an object that includes `[Body](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3")`
object and an algorithm that takes a[byte sequence](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") and returns a JavaScript value or throws an
exceptionconvertBytesToJSValue, runs these steps:


1.       If object is [unusable](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3"), then return [a promise rejected with](#fetch-elsewhere-ongoing "Content Security Policy Level 3") a `[TypeError](#ipr "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.

2.       Let promise be [a new promise](#index "Content Security Policy Level 3").

3. Let errorSteps given error be to [reject](#index-defined-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") promise with error.      
4. Let successSteps given a [byte sequence](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3") data be to [resolve](#normative "Content Security Policy Level 3") promise with the result of running convertBytesToJSValue with data. If that threw an exception, then run errorSteps with that exception.      
5.       If object’s [body](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") is null, then run successSteps with an empty [byte sequence](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3").

6.       Otherwise, [fully read](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") object’s [body](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") given successSteps, errorSteps, and object’s [relevant global object](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly").

7.       Return promise.


The *`arrayBuffer()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](#http-origin "The Web Origin Concept") with [this](#biblio-origin "The Web Origin Concept") and the following step given a[byte sequence](#preface "Infra Standard")
bytes: return the result of [creating](#preface "Infra Standard") an`[ArrayBuffer](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")`
from bytes in [this](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")’s [relevant realm](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard").

The above method can reject with a `[RangeError](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")`.


The *`blob()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](#infrastructure "Infra Standard") with [this](#biblio-infra "Infra Standard") and the following step given a[byte sequence](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF")
bytes: return a `[Blob](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF")`
whose contents are bytesand whose `[type](#biblio-encoding "Encoding Standard")` attribute
is the result of [get the MIME type](#biblio-http "HTML Standard") with[this](#biblio-mimesniff "MIME Sniffing Standard").


The *`bytes()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](#biblio-webtransport "WebTransport") with [this](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF") and the following step given a[byte sequence](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF")
bytes: return the result of [creating](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") a`[Uint8Array](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism")`
from bytes in [this](#biblio-cookies "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism")’s [relevant realm](#biblio-cookies "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism").

The above method can reject with a `[RangeError](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "HTTP Semantics")`.


The *`formData()`* method steps are to return the result ofrunning [consume body](#concept-request "Resource Timing") with [this](#concept-request "Resource Timing") and the following steps given a[byte sequence](#concept-request "Resource Timing")
bytes:


1.       Let mimeType be the result of [get the MIME type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") with [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").

2.       If mimeType is non-null, then switch on mimeType’s  [essence](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") and run the corresponding steps:  
"`multipart/form-data`"                 

1.           Parse bytes, using the value of the \``boundary`\` parameter from      mimeType, per the rules set forth in      *Returning Values from Forms: multipart/form-data*. [[RFC7578]](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")      

	Each part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header contains a      \``filename`\` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") whose      value is a `[File](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")` object whose contents are the contents of the part. The `[name](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")`      attribute of the `[File](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")` object must have the value of the \``filename`\` parameter      of the part. The `[type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Resource Timing")` attribute of the `[File](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")` object must have the value of the      \``Content-Type`\` header of the part if the part has such header, and      \``text/plain`\` (the default defined by [[RFC7578]](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") section 4.4) otherwise.      

	Each part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header does not contain a      \``filename`\` parameter must be parsed into an [entry](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") whose      value is the [UTF-8 decoded without BOM](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") content of the      part. This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a      \``Content-Type`\` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a      \``charset`\` parameter.      

	A part whose \``Content-Disposition`\` header contains a      \``name`\` parameter whose value is \``_charset_`\` is parsed like any other      part. It does not change the encoding.     

2.           If that fails for some reason, then [throw](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")`.     

3.           Return a new `[FormData](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing")` object, appending each [entry](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing"),     resulting from the parsing operation, to its [entry list](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing").    


The above is a rough approximation of what is needed for    \``multipart/form-data`\`, a more detailed parsing specification is to be written.    Volunteers welcome.   
"`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`"                 

1.           Let entries be the result of  [parsing](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")     bytes.     

2.           Return a new `[FormData](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")` object whose [entry list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is     entries.    


-       [Throw](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing")`.


The *`json()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") with [this](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") and [parse JSON from bytes](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

The above method can reject with a `[SyntaxError](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")`.


The *`text()`* method steps are to return the resultof running [consume body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing") with [this](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing") and [UTF-8 decode](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing").


The *`textStream()`* method steps are:


1.       If this is [unusable](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then throw a `[TypeError](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")`.

2.       If this’s [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing") is null:  


1.         Let emptyStream be a new `[ReadableStream](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing")` in [this](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing")’s   [relevant realm](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").   

2.         [Set up](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") emptyStream.   

3.         [Close](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") emptyStream.   

4.         Return emptyStream.  


-       Let stream be [this](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s [body](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s [stream](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing").

-       Let decoder be a new `[TextDecoderStream](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")` object in [this](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")’s [relevant realm](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").

-       [Set up](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") decoder with  [UTF-8](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing").  

	This is done regardless of the presence or the value of a  \``Content-Type`\` header and regardless of the presence or the value of a  \``charset`\` parameter.

-       Return the result of stream, [piped through](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") decoder.
