"sharedworker"*, *dfn-paneled has-dfn-panel
"style"*, *algorithm
"text"*, *algorithm
"track"*, *example
"video"*, *example
"worker"*, *example
"xslt"* };enum *example
RequestMode* { *example
"navigate"*, *example
"same-origin"*, *example
"no-cors"*, *example
"cors"* };enum *example
RequestCredentials* { *example
"omit"*, *example
"same-origin"*, *example
"include"* };enum *example
RequestCache* { *example
"default"*, *example
"no-store"*, *example
"reload"*, *tablenote
"no-cache"*, *tablenote
"force-cache"*, *tablenote
"only-if-cached"* };enum *h-entry status-LS
RequestRedirect* { *h-entry status-LS
"follow"*, *h-entry status-LS
"error"*, *h-entry status-LS
"manual"* };enum *h-entry status-LS
RequestDuplex* { *h-entry status-LS
"half"* };enum *h-entry status-LS
RequestPriority* { *h-entry status-LS
"high"*, *h-entry status-LS
"low"*, *h-entry status-LS
"auto"* };```
   
"`serviceworker`" is omitted from[`RequestDestination`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") as it cannot be observed from JavaScript. Implementationswill still need to support it as a [destination](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
"`websocket`" and"`webtransport`" are omitted from [`RequestMode`](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") as they cannot beused or observed from JavaScript.
   
A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object has an associated*request* (a [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")).
   
A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object also has an associated *headers* (null 
or a`[Headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object), initially null.
   
A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object has an associated *signal* (null or 
an `[AbortSignal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`object), initially null.
   
A `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is its[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
---
       `request = new [Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")(input [,
 init])`          
Returns a new request whose `[url](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` property is input 
if  input is a string, and input’s `[url](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` if input 
is a  `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object.  
     
The init argument is an object whose properties can be set as follows:
           `[method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string to set request’s 
`[method](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.         `[headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` 
        A `[Headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, an object literal, or an array 
of two-item arrays to set   request’s `[headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`.    
     `[body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A `[BodyInit](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` 
object or null to set request’s [body](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         `[referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` 
        A string whose value is a same-origin URL, "`about:client`", or the empty string,   to 
set request’s [referrer](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").         `[referrerPolicy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` 
        A [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") to set request’s `[referrerPolicy](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string to indicate 
whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted to same-origin   URLs. Sets 
request’s `[mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`. If input is a string, it defaults 
to   "`cors`".         `[credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating 
whether credentials will be sent with the request always, never, or only   when sent 
to a same-origin URL — as well as whether any credentials sent back in the response 
  will be used always, never, or only when received from a same-origin URL. Sets 
  request’s `[credentials](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`. If input is a string, it defaults 
to   "`same-origin`".         `[cache](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating 
how the request will interact with the browser’s cache to set   request’s `[cache](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[redirect](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A string indicating 
whether request follows redirects, results in an error upon   encountering a redirect, 
or returns the redirect (in an opaque fashion). Sets   request’s `[redirect](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[integrity](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A cryptographic 
hash of the resource to be fetched by request. Sets   request’s `[integrity](https://whatwg.org/
"subtitle")`.         `[keepalive](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`         A boolean to 
set request’s `[keepalive](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle")`.         `[signal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")` 
        An `[AbortSignal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "You can also press the 'y' key")` 
to set request’s `[signal](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y' key")`. 
        `[window](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/commits "You can also press the 'y' key")` 
        Can only be null. Used to disassociate request from any `[Window](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key")`.         `[duplex](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")` 
        "`half`" is the only valid value and it is for initiating a half-duplex fetch 
  (i.e., the user agent sends the entire request before processing the response). 
  "`full`" is reserved for future use, for initiating a full-duplex fetch (i.e., the 
  user agent can process the response before sending the entire request). This member 
needs to be   set when `[body](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
is a `[ReadableStream](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object. See   [issue #1254](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3") for defining  
"`full`".         `[priority](#goals "Content Security Policy Level 3")`         A string 
to set request’s [priority](#preface "Content Security Policy Level 3").        
   `request . [method](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns request’s 
HTTP method, which is "`GET`" by default.     `request . [url](#http "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the URL of request as a string.     `request . [headers](#terminology-headers "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns a `[Headers](#bodies "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object consisting of 
the headers associated with request. Note that headers added in the network layer 
by the user agent will not be accounted for in this object, e.g., the "`Host`" header. 
    `request . [destination](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns the kind of 
resource requested by request, e.g., "`document`" or "`script`".     `request . [referrer](#fetch-groups "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the referrer of request. Its value can be a same-origin URL if explicitly 
set in init, the empty string to indicate no referrer, and "`about:client`" when defaulting to 
the global’s default. This is used during fetching to determine the value of the 
\``Referer`\` header of the request being made.     `request . [referrerPolicy](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the referrer policy associated with request. This is used during fetching 
to compute the value of the request’s referrer.     `request . [mode](#http-extensions "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the [mode](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with request, 
which is a string indicating whether the request will use CORS, or will be restricted 
to same-origin URLs.     `request . [credentials](#set-cookie-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the [credentials mode](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating whether credentials will be sent with the 
request always, never, or only when sent to a same-origin URL.     `request . [cache](#general "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the [cache mode](#http-requests "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating how the request will interact with the browser’s 
cache when fetching.     `request . [redirect](#http-new-header-syntax "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns the [redirect mode](#cors-protocol-and-credentials "Content Security Policy Level 3") associated with
request, which is a string indicating how redirects for the request will be handled 
during fetching. A [request](#cors-protocol-exceptions "Content Security Policy Level 3") will follow redirects by default.     `request . [integrity](#content-length-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns request’s subresource integrity metadata, which is a cryptographic 
hash of the resource being fetched. Its value consists of multiple hashes separated 
by whitespace. [[SRI]](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")     `request . [keepalive](#should-response-to-request-be-blocked-due-to-nosniff? "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request can outlive the global in 
which it was created.     `request . [isReloadNavigation](#sec-purpose-header "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
    Returns a boolean indicating whether or not request is for a reload navigation. 
    `request . [isHistoryNavigation](#fetching "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns a boolean indicating 
whether or not request is for a history navigation (a.k.a. back-foward navigation). 
    `request . [signal](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns the signal 
associated with request, which is an `[AbortSignal](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
object indicating whether or not request has been aborted, and its abort event handler. 
    `request . [duplex](#http-network-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")`     Returns "`half`", 
meaning the fetch will be half-duplex (i.e., the user agent sends the entire request 
before processing the response). In future, it could also return "`full`", meaning the 
fetch will be full-duplex (i.e., the user agent can process the response before sending 
the entire request) to indicate that the fetch will be full-duplex. See [issue #1254](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") for defining
"`full`".     `request . [clone](#deferred-fetch-quota "Content Security Policy Level 3")()`     
     
Returns a clone of request.
      
---
   

    
To *create* a `[Request](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object, given a[request](#body-mixin "Content Security Policy Level 3")
request, [headers guard](#request-class "Content Security Policy Level 3") guard,`[AbortSignal](#fetch-method "Content Security Policy Level 3")` 
object signal, and [realm](#garbage-collection "Content Security Policy Level 3") realm:
   

     1.       Let requestObject be a [new](#data-urls "Content Security Policy Level 3") `[Request](#background-reading "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object with realm. 
     
2.       Set requestObject’s [request](#http-header-layer-division "Content Security Policy Level 3") to request. 
     
3.       Set requestObject’s [headers](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") to a [new](#basic-safe-cors-protocol-setup "Content Security Policy Level 3") `[Headers](#cors-protocol-and-http-caches "Content Security Policy Level 3")` object with realm, whose [headers list](#websocket-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") is request’s [headers list](#fetch-elsewhere "Content Security Policy Level 3") and [guard](#fetch-elsewhere-request "Content Security Policy Level 3") is guard. 
     
4.       Set requestObject’s [signal](#fetch-elsewhere-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") to signal. 
     
5.       Return requestObject.
    

      
---
   

    
The*`new Request(input, init)`*constructor steps are:
   

     1.       Let request be null. 
     
2.       Let fallbackMode be null. 
     
3.       Let baseURL be [this](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [relevant settings object](#references "Content Security Policy Level 3")’s [API base URL](#informative "Content Security Policy Level 3"). 
     
4.       Let signal be null. 
     
5.       If input is a string, then:  
     

       1.         Let parsedURL be the result of   [parsing](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") input with   baseURL.   
       
2.         If parsedURL is failure, then [throw](#biblio-csp "Content Security Policy Level 3") a `[TypeError](#biblio-fetch-metadata "Content Security Policy Level 3")`.   
       
3.         If parsedURL [includes credentials](#biblio-sw "Service Workers Nightly"), then   [throw](#biblio-mix "Mixed Content") a `[TypeError](#biblio-referrer "Upgrade Insecure Requests")`.   
       
4.         Set request to a new [request](#http-origin "The Web Origin Concept") whose [URL](#biblio-origin "The Web Origin Concept") is   parsedURL.   
       
5.         Set fallbackMode to "`cors`".  
      

     
-       Otherwise:  
     

       1.         [Assert](#preface "Infra Standard"): input is a `[Request](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")` object.   
       
2.         Set request to input’s   [request](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard").   
       
3.         Set signal to input’s [signal](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard").  
      

     
-       Let origin be [this](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")’s [relevant settings object](#dom-global-fetch "Infra Standard")’s [origin](#infrastructure "Infra Standard"). 
     
-       Let traversableForUserPrompts be "`client`". 
     
-       If request’s [traversable for user prompts](#biblio-infra "Infra Standard") is an [environment settings object](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF") and its [origin](#biblio-abnf "Augmented BNF for Syntax Specifications: ABNF") is [same origin](#biblio-encoding "Encoding Standard") with origin, then set traversableForUserPrompts to request’s [traversable for user prompts](#biblio-mimesniff "MIME Sniffing Standard"). 
     
-       If init["`[window](#biblio-url "URL Standard")`"] [exists](#biblio-webidl "Web IDL Standard") and is non-null, then [throw](#biblio-webtransport "WebTransport") a `[TypeError](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF")`. 
     
-       If init["`[window](#biblio-rfc7405 "Case-Sensitive String Support in ABNF")`"] [exists](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism"), then set traversableForUserPrompts to "`no-traversable`". 
     
-       Set request to a new [request](#authentication-entry "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism") with the following properties:  
             [URL](#biblio-tls "Cookies: HTTP State Management Mechanism")          request’s [URL](#biblio-http "HTTP Semantics").          [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")          request’s [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing").          [header list](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing")          A copy of request’s [header list](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing").          [unsafe-request flag](#concept-request "Resource Timing")          Set.          [client](#concept-request "Resource Timing")          [This](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")’s [relevant settings object](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").          [traversable for user prompts](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          traversableForUserPrompts.          [internal priority](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          request’s [internal priority](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").          [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          request’s [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"). The propagation of the   [origin](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") is only significant for navigation requests being handled by a service   worker. In this scenario a request can have an origin that is different from the current   client.          [referrer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          request’s [referrer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").          [referrer policy](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          request’s [referrer policy](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Resource Timing").          [mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")          request’s [mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").          [credentials mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")          request’s [credentials mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").          [cache mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")          request’s [cache mode](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing").          [redirect mode](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")          request’s [redirect mode](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing").          [integrity metadata](#concept-response "Resource Timing")          request’s [integrity metadata](#concept-response "Resource Timing").          [keepalive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")          request’s [keepalive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing").          [reload-navigation flag](#concept-fetch "Resource Timing")          request’s [reload-navigation flag](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing").          [history-navigation flag](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")          request’s [history-navigation flag](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing").          [URL list](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing")          A [clone](#fetch-timing-info "Resource Timing") of request’s [URL list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing").          [initiator type](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing")          "`fetch`".             
-       If init [is not empty](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing"), then:  
     

       1.         If request’s [mode](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") is   "`navigate`", then set it to "`same-origin`".   
       
2.         Unset request’s [reload-navigation flag](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing").   
       
3.         Unset request’s [history-navigation flag](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing").   
       
4.         Set request’s [origin](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to "`client`".   
       
5.         Set request’s [referrer](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to "`client`".   
       
6.         Set request’s [referrer policy](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to the empty string.   
       
7.         Set request’s [URL](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Resource Timing") to request’s   [current URL](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing").   
       
8.         Set request’s [URL list](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing") to « request’s   [URL](#fetch-controller-report-timing-steps "Resource Timing") ».  
      

      This is done to ensure that when a service worker "redirects" a request, e.g., from  an image in a cross-origin style sheet, and makes modifications, it no longer appears to come from  the original source (i.e., the cross-origin style sheet), but instead from the service worker that  "redirected" the request. This is important as the original source might not even be able to  generate the same kind of requests as the service worker. Services that trust the original source  could therefore be exploited were this not done, although that is somewhat farfetched. 
     
-       If init["`[referrer](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing"), then:  
     

       1.         Let referrer be init["`[referrer](#fetch-controller-next-manual-redirect-steps "Resource Timing")`"].   
       
2.         If referrer is the empty string, then set request’s   [referrer](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to "`no-referrer`".   
       
3.         Otherwise:    
       

         1.           Let parsedReferrer be the result of [parsing](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing")     referrer with baseURL.     
         
2.           If parsedReferrer is failure, then [throw](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](#fetch-controller-full-timing-info "Resource Timing")`.     
         
3.           If one of the following is true      
                     4.             parsedReferrer’s [scheme](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is "`about`" and       [path](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is the string "`client`"       
           
5.             parsedReferrer’s [origin](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") is not [same origin](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing") with       origin      
          
          then set request’s [referrer](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing") to "`client`".     
         
6.           Otherwise, set request’s [referrer](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") to     parsedReferrer.    
        

      

     
-       
If init["`[referrerPolicy](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource
Timing")`"] [exists](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing"), then set
request’s [referrer policy](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structuredserialize "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
Let mode be init["`[mode](#fetch-controller-serialized-abort-reason "Resource Timing")`"] 
if it [exists](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing"), and
fallbackMode otherwise. 
     
-       
If mode is "`navigate`", then [throw](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#sec-list-and-record-specification-type "Resource
Timing")`. 
     
-       
If mode is non-null, set request’s [mode](https://tc39.es/ecma262/#realm "Resource Timing") to
mode. 
     
-       
If init["`[credentials](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#aborterror "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](https://webidl.spec.whatwg.org/#idl-DOMException "Resource Timing"), then set
request’s [credentials mode](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
If init["`[cache](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/structured-data.html#structureddeserialize "Resource
Timing")`"] [exists](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing"), then set request’s 
[cache mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
If request’s [cache mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is "`only-if-cached`" and request’s 
[mode](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") is *not* "`same-origin`", then [throw](#fetch-controller "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](#fetch-controller-state "Resource
Timing")`. 
     
-       
If init["`[redirect](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing"), then set
request’s [redirect mode](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
If init["`[integrity](#fetch-params "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing"), then set
request’s [integrity metadata](#fetch-params-controller "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
If init["`[keepalive](#fetch-controller-state "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing"), then set
request’s [keepalive](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") to it. 
     
-       
If init["`[method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing")`"] [exists](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing"), then:  
     

       1.         Let method be init["`[method](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing")`"].   
       
2.         If method is not a [method](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") or method is a   [forbidden method](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"), then [throw](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") a `[TypeError](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")`.   
       
3.         [Normalize](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") method.   
       
4.         Set request’s [method](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to method.  
      

     
-       
If init["`[signal](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")`"] 
[exists](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"), then set signal 
to it. 
     
-       
If init["`[priority](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")`"] 
[exists](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing"), then:  
     

       1.         If request’s [internal priority](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") is not null, then update   request’s [internal priority](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") in an [implementation-defined](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")   manner.   
       
2.         Otherwise, set request’s [priority](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing") to   init["`[priority](https://w3c.github.io/hr-time/#dom-domhighrestimestamp "Resource Timing")`"].  
      

     
-       
Set [this](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s [request](#connection-timing-info "Resource Timing") to
request. 
     
-       
Let signals be « signal » if signal is non-null; otherwise « ». 
     
-             
Set [this](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing")’s [signal](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") to the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](https://w3c.github.io/ServiceWorker/#service-worker-timing-info "Resource Timing") from
signals, using `[AbortSignal](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing")` and [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing")’s [relevant realm](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#list "Resource Timing"). 
     
-       
Set [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")’s [headers](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") to a [new](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing") `[Headers](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")` 
object with [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct "Resource Timing")’s [relevant realm](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#struct-item "Resource Timing"), whose [header list](#biblio-resource-timing "Resource Timing") is
request’s [header list](#biblio-navigation-timing "Navigation Timing") and [guard](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is
"`request`". 
     
-       
If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is 
"`no-cors`", then:  
     

       1.         If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")’s [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") is not a   [CORS-safelisted method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy"), then [throw](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy") a `[TypeError](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-string "Referrer Policy")`.   
       
2.         Set [this](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy")’s [headers](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy")’s [guard](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy") to   "`request-no-cors`".  
      

     
-       
If init [is not empty](#fetch-timing-info "Referrer Policy"), then:  
      
The headers are sanitized as they might contain headers that are not allowed by this 
 mode. Otherwise, they were previously sanitized or are unmodified since they were 
set by a  privileged API.  
     

       1.         Let headers be a copy of [this](#fetch-timing-info-start-time "Referrer Policy")’s [headers](#fetch-timing-info-end-time "Referrer Policy") and its   associated [header list](#fetch-timing-info-end-time "Referrer Policy").   
       
2.         If init["`[headers](#fetch-timing-info-render-blocking "Referrer Policy")`"] [exists](#fetch-timing-info-render-blocking "Referrer Policy"), then set   headers to init["`[headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy")`"].   
       
3.         Empty [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy")’s [headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#global-object "Referrer Policy")’s [header list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy").   
       
4.         If headers is a `[Headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy")` object, then [for each](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#parallel-queue "Referrer Policy")   header of its [header list](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/infrastructure.html#enqueue-the-following-steps "Referrer Policy"), [append](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy")   header to [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#queue-a-global-task "Referrer Policy")’s [headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#networking-task-source "Referrer Policy").   
       
5.         Otherwise, [fill](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#networking-task-source "Referrer Policy") [this](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object "Referrer Policy")’s [headers](https://html.spec.whatwg.org/multipage/webappapis.html#environment-settings-object "Referrer Policy") with   headers.  
      

     
-       
Let inputBody be input’s [request](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy")’s [body](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy") if
input is a `[Request](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer Policy")` 
object; otherwise null. 
     
-       
If either init["`[body](https://w3c.github.io/webdriver-bidi/#webdriver-bidi-network-is-offline "Referrer
Policy")`"] [exists](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") and is non-null or
inputBody is non-null, and request’s [method](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") is
\``GET`\` or \``HEAD`\`, then [throw](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer Policy") a `[TypeError](https://github.com/whatwg/infra/issues/201 "Referrer
Policy")`. 
     
-       
Let initBody be null. 
     
-       
If init["`[body](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy")`"] [exists](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url "Referrer Policy") and is non-null, then:  
     

       1.         Let bodyWithType be the result of [extracting](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy")   init["`[body](https://url.spec.whatwg.org/#concept-url-scheme "Referrer Policy")`"], with [*keepalive*](#local-scheme "Referrer Policy")   set to request’s [keepalive](#biblio-referrer "Referrer Policy").   
       
2.         Set initBody to bodyWithType’s [body](#http-scheme "HTML Standard").   
       
3.         Let type be bodyWithType’s [type](#http-scheme "HTML Standard").   
       
4.         If type is non-null and [this](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")’s [headers](#http-scheme "HTML Standard")’s   [header list](#http-scheme "HTML Standard") [does not contain](#fetch-scheme "HTML Standard")   \``Content-Type`\`, then [append](#http "HTML Standard") (\``Content-Type`\`,   type) to [this](#http "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [headers](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").  
      

     
-       
Let inputOrInitBody be initBody if it is non-null; otherwise inputBody. 
     
-       
If inputOrInitBody is non-null and inputOrInitBody’s  [source](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is null, then:  
     

       1.         If initBody is non-null and init["`[duplex](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")`"] does   not [exist](#http-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."), then throw a `[TypeError](https://mimesniff.spec.whatwg.org/#mime-type "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")`.   
       
2.         If [this](#http-tab-or-space "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [mode](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#ascii-whitespace "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is neither   "`same-origin`" nor "`cors`", then throw a `[TypeError](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")`.   
       
3.         Set [this](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s   [use-CORS-preflight flag](#http-newline-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").  
      

     
-       
Let finalBody be inputOrInitBody. 
     
-       
If initBody is null and inputBody is non-null, then:  
     

       1.         If inputBody is [unusable](#http-tab-or-space-byte "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."), then [throw](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") a `[TypeError](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")`.   
       
2.         Set finalBody to the result of [creating a proxy](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") for   inputBody.  
      

     
-       
Set [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [body](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") to
finalBody.
    

      
The *`method`* getter steps are to return [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s[request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [method](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#collect-a-sequence-of-code-points "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").
   
The *`url`* getter steps are to return [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s[request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [URL](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default."), [serialized](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").
   
The *`headers`* getter steps are to return[this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [headers](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#code-point "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").
   
The *`destination`* getter are to return [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s[request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#assert "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [destination](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#iteration-break "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.").
   

    
The *`referrer`* getter steps are:
   

     1.       If [this](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](#example-http-quoted-string "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [referrer](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`no-referrer`", then return the empty string. 
     
2.       If [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [referrer](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.") is "`client`", then return "`about:client`". 
     
3.       Return [this](https://infra.spec.whatwg.org/#string-position-variable "Multiple vendors' web servers enable HTTP TRACE method by default.")’s [request](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css)’s [referrer](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard-shared-with-dev.css), [serialized](https://resources.whatwg.org/standard.css "subtitle").
    

      
The *`referrerPolicy`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [referrer policy](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`mode`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`credentials`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [credentials mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`cache`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [cache mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`redirect`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [redirect mode](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`integrity`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [integrity metadata](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`keepalive`* getter steps are to return[this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [keepalive](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`isReloadNavigation`* getter steps are to returntrue if [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [reload-navigation flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is set;otherwise false.
   
The *`isHistoryNavigation`* getter steps are to returntrue if [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [history-navigation flag](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") isset; otherwise false.
   
The *`signal`* getter steps are to return [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s[signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
[This](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is always initialized in the[constructor](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") and when[cloning](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle").
   
The *`duplex`* getter steps are to return"`half`".
   
---
   

    
The *`clone()`* method steps are:
   

     1.       If [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is [unusable](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), then [throw](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[TypeError](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")`. 
     
2.       Let clonedRequest be the result of [cloning](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
3.       [Assert](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"): [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") is non-null. 
     
4.             Let clonedSignal be the result of [creating a dependent abort signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") from « [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [signal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") », using `[AbortSignal](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` and [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"). 
     
5.       Let clonedRequestObject be the result of [creating](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle") a `[Request](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")` object, given clonedRequest, [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [headers](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [guard](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle"), clonedSignal and [this](https://whatwg.org/ "subtitle")’s [relevant realm](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch "subtitle"). 
     
6.       Return clonedRequestObject.
    

      
### 5.5. Response class

```[[Exposed](https://github.com/whatwg/fetch/issues "You can also press the 'y'
key")=(Window,Worker)]interface *p-summary
Response* {  [constructor](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/tree/master/fetch "You can also press the 'y'
key")(optional [BodyInit](https://github.com/web-platform-tests/wpt/labels/fetch "Japanese")? *p-summary
body* = null, optional [ResponseInit](https://ko.htmlspecs.com/fetch/ "Korean") *no-num no-ref no-toc

[error](#infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")();  [[NewObject](#url "Content Security Policy Level 3")]

3")([USVString](#statuses "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno
url*, optional [unsigned short](#miscellaneous "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno
status* = 302);  [[NewObject](#connections "Content Security Policy Level 3")] static [Response](#network-partition-keys "Content Security Policy Level 3")
[json](#http-cache-partitions "Content Security Policy Level 3")([any](#port-blocking "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno
data*, optional [ResponseInit](#cookies "Content Security Policy Level 3") *secno
init* = {});  readonly attribute [ResponseType](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3")
[type](#cookie-infrastructure "Content Security Policy Level 3");  readonly attribute
[USVString](#http-cors-protocol "Content Security Policy Level 3") [url](#general "Content Security Policy Level 3");

[[NewObject](#override-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")] [Response](#scheme-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")
[clone](#http-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")();};[Response](#http-redirect-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3")
includes [Body](#http-network-or-cache-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3");dictionary
*secno
ResponseInit* {  [unsigned short](#cors-preflight-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") *content
status* = 200;  [ByteString](#deferred-fetch "Content Security Policy Level 3") *toc
